The youngster spread both hands, after everyone saw the bubbling red light disappear, they saw the youngster’s hands had transformed into monkey tail brown bear claws.

During many late nights, the brown bear youngster diligently mined in a cave.

The people no longer withdrew, as their fear and nervousness began to reduce, they started to look in amazement.

Seeing the bear claws, Cang Xu seemed to think of some question and sank into contemplation.

“I am a blade spider.”

The youngster transformed spider blades.

In the forest, the blade spider youngster sank into vexation: “It seems weaving spider webs also needs diligent training!”

“Heavens. That’s the truth!” Someone murmured as he recognized it, that was the sword the youngster commonly used to battle the magic beasts.

“I am also a flying squirrel capable of electrical discharges.”

Electricity began to curl around the youngster’s transformed spider blade.

In order to emit electricity, he had trained for a long time and didn’t hesitate to capture live flying squirrels to be shocked by so he could find the electrical discharge method.

“That’s right, I am a beast, a monster.” The youngster had an extremely heavy expression, at that moment, he felt he was naked in a world of ice and snow.

Like he thought, the people looked at him with strong fear and rejection and wanted to flee from him.

But different from his expectations, besides fear, rejection, and evasiveness, there were many other emotions. This caused many to have complex expressions.

No sounds transmitted from the fourth floor.

This was also the first time Jia Sha had seen the youngster’s transformation, and he felt very surprised.

The monstrous youngster continued: “Although I am a beast and a monster, I wanted to become a knight, therefore I always used the knight’s creed to judge myself.”

“I wanted to become someone beloved by the gods, therefore I always prayed, however Emperor Sheng Ming never responded to me.”

“I wanted to become a noble, and the sole heir of a great clan. As a result, I always urged myself to strive in the restoration of my clan.”

“I wanted to become a leader, therefore I tried hard to charge ahead, and uphold fairness in my heart and soul.”

“How much I wish I was these things!”

“I almost believed myself.”

“But I am not.”

“The memories in my mind belong to someone else.”

“My name is another’s.”

“My fiancé also belongs to another.”

“Since awakening on this island, I have lived in a lie.”

The youngster forced a smile: “Did you know when I beastified for the first time, I was shocked!”

“I suspected it was a bloodline awakening and mistakenly suspected it was some kind of body transformation potion.”

“When I discovered at every step that I could transform into many beasts, I endured suffering.”

“Fear, doubt, indecisiveness……I asked myself, why did I turn out this way?”

“I didn’t want to worry Zi Di, thus I didn’t dare to speak the truth.

“How I wish this was a nightmare, however it really happened to me. I wanted to forget, however I could not, because the harsh environment always forced me to use my abilities.”

“Gradually, I discovered parts of their bodies, was a part of me. I loathed and evaded them, I loathed and shunned myself.”

“I sympathized with the giant, because in him, I see myself.”

“When my comrades beastified, I was the one most afraid. I especially feared a day when I would lose my rationality, become brutal, and kill those at my side.”

The youngster looked at Zong Ge and frankly said: “Someone said I deceived you all, correct, I deceived everyone. If possible, I still wanted to deceive everyone for a lifetime. If possible, I wanted to deceive myself for the rest of my life.”

“Because I didn’t want to be a monster! I don’t want to be a beastified person, a body double, or a tool! I don’t want to be rejected, hated, or discriminated against.”

Everyone was emotionally moved.

The youngster bowed his head, looked at his hands, and bitterly laughed: “Actually there were many flaws during my journey, but why haven’t I detected them until now?”

“Because I wanted to deceive myself more.”

“I deceived myself into thinking I was a noble knight that had fine amor and sat upon a white horse.

“I deceived myself into believing I was a noble that had a castle, a summer manor, beautiful subjects, and a financially successful fiancé.”

“I deceived myself into thinking the gods cared for and appreciated me, I thought I proved I had firm convictions.”

“I failed.”

“As you can see, I am no one.”

“I am nothing!”

“Now I can only tell myself: hey, you idiot, don’t deceive yourself, abandon these ridiculous, pathetic, and unrealistic fantasies, you are nothing! Recognize reality, you were never a normal man, you are a beastified person, a monster.”

Saying this, the youngster’s eyes reddened.

He had revealed the truth.

Everyone was silent.

Everyone quietly looked at the youngster.

Their vigilant expressions disappeared.

“Haha, you know yourself well, body double.” Zhen Jin jeered from the fourth floor.

The people immediately puckered their brows, and many showed their hatred.

But before he continued, the youngster’s voice suddenly increased and changed the topic: “Although I am nothing, who thinks that of me?”

“I am nothing, but can’t I act?”

“I am nothing, but can’t I try?”

The people’s hearts shook.

“Hmph, wishful thinking.” Jia Sha was exceedingly displeased and denounced, “At birth, a man’s identity and entire life is decided. You should recognize reality, know your place, and be happy with what you can obtain. Greed, desire, and lusting after things that don’t belong to you will inevitably end in miserable darkness. This is your greatest original sin, and those that hold this opinion are the root of society’s upheaval. Why do you have the nerve to say this? Perhaps now you can still receive redemption and forgiveness!”

Everyone’s brows wrinkled deeply.

The body double ignored Jia Sha and continued: “If there is an opportunity, I want to become a knight, a lord, and a divine scion knight. Don’t tell me you don’t want these things!”

Everyone’s body and mind quaked.

“If you didn’t want these things, why are you heading to the Wilderness Continent?! This journey and ocean crossing is very risky, why are you going to a place filled with war and hazards?!”

“I am a monster, I have been rejected, but what about you? Don’t tell me none of you have not been excluded, disdained, hated, crushed by big wigs, or cheated in life? Don’t you have dreams that have tormented you?!”

The youngster’s series of rapid questions created absolute silence.

The youngster deeply breathed, slowed down his tone, and deeply said: “We are actually the same.”

“We were born mediocre.” The youngster looked at Bai Ya, causing Bai Ya to blush and look emotional.

“We were rejected and loathed.” The youngster looked at the giant. The giant’s eyelids then trembled a little.

“We were betrayed by a close relative.” The youngster looked at Cang Xu. The old scholar narrowed his eyes.

“We cannot always get impartial treatment.” The youngster looked at the half beastman. The half beastman’s breathing had already become rough and heavy.

“We are also guilty, and incapable of forgiving ourselves." The youngster looked at Lan Zao. Since the youngster had reawakened, Lan Zao had been kneeling down, but now he looked up with hope.

“We were spurred, banished, wanted, and ridiculed by others. So what?”

“Should we not think about it?”

“Should we not do something about it?"

“Reality has already made us suffer, and it is unbearable to have a terrible life. We only want to become better and do better.”

“In fact, we are always trying.”

“We were stranded on this island together and fought together. In order to hunt for food and water, we were poisoned. We struggled in the desert, suffered pursuing beast groups, beat back magic beasts from our camps, trekked a long way, gathered supplies along the way, constructed a new ship in the ravine, and broke through the alchemy factory’s obstructions.”

The youngster paced in front the crowd as he repeated his time with them.

What is our purpose?” The youngster opened his arms and asked.

“We want to survive.”

“But that is an insufficient reason!”

“Far from enough.”

“We also want to live better!”

“I’ve always believed this, and I don’t believe you don’t want this!”

“Shut up, shut up. You are a devil, you are stirring them up with false statements, you are a disgrace. You are too guilty, if your wrongdoing continues, redemption is impossible!” Jia Sha shouted; the youngster’s words had made him extremely angry.

The youngster laughed: “Devil? Yes, I am one!”

He clapped his chest and brazenly admitted it.

Then, he pointed into the crowd and resonated: “But it isn’t just me. Everyone is a devil, a beast!”

“We want to survive, we want to live comfortably, we want abundant food and water, we want the identities and honor of others, we want divine artifacts, and we want overflowing contribution. Therefore, you are also like us Lord Priest!”

In an instant, Jia Sha’s pupils shrank.

The youngster shouted: “On this island, we got lost and were pursued like dying dogs. We collapsed from hard work, with our only hope being the new ship. For countless nights, we have prayed together that the weather would clear tomorrow.”

He continued to stomp his feet and point his finger.

“This is War Merchant’s Mystifying Monster Island. Not only are there artificial magic beasts here, there are also devils and monsters.”

“We seem to have lost our direction in life, but as a matter of fact, our hearts understand how we want to move forward.”

“Everyone, listen to your heart’s voice!” The youngster emotionally slapped his chest, “What do you want in the end? Is it to obediently die here? Do you want to surrender? Do you trust this priest? Like a dog wagging its tail, do you want to beg him for your life? After he swallows all of the contribution, like a master eating everything, will you gnaw on the bone he tosses to you?”

“Bone, this giant doesn’t eat bone, this giant eats meat.” In a daze, the giant opened his eyes. When he woke up, he saw the youngster and everyone else, and for a moment, he didn’t react.

“I don’t have the qualifications to lead you. But I want everyone to listen carefully to their heart, what do you really want?”

“To yield and stop, or to rise vigorously and rebel?”

The crowd was deathly silent.

But this deathly silence was somewhat different from the last one.

As long as one carefully listened, one could hear the people’s beating hearts and coarse breathing.

“Father, you’re still alive!” The giant shouted, and he got up with ecstasy, however because he moved too quickly and was still dizzy, he couldn’t stand up and had to kneel on the ground.

The body double smiled at him, then looked at everyone again.

He didn’t speak again.

He was waiting.

After he waited!

“Master, no matter who you are, you will always be my master!” Lan Zao shouted as he got up.

“You saved my life many times. I want to follow you, your Lordship.” Bai Ya exclaimed.

“Compared to Zhen Jin, I have always believed that you are more fit to be a noble and a knight.” Cang Xu slowly opened his mouth as he wiped his singular lens.

“You are a hero, our hero.” Mu Ban took out a rapid fire crossbow, causing others around him to subconsciously move away.

Zong Ge took a deep breath then deeply said: “In my entire life, this is the most touching speech I have ever listened to. Then let us……fight together one more time”

The giant stood up, and Zong Ge walked over with Tripleblade following closely.

Lan Zao, Cang Xu, Bai Ya……

Everyone came together at last.

In the end, they converged at the youngster side once again.

But this time.

The youngster was no longer Zhen Jin, a templar knight, a noble, or a divine scion knight.

But……a monster!

Chapter 208: Charging to Meet Death!

Seeing everyone gathering at his side, the monstrous youngster smiled.

However, his current form was terrifying and wasn’t human at all, thus the smile more resembled an evil grin.

He roared and pointed to the ceiling: “The imperial flag is there, noble honor also enveloped that place, and perhaps the gods’ concern is there too.”

“But that place holds our means to survival and the treasure we desire!”

The monstrous youngster raised his head and shouted, then he took the lead to rush out of the safe zone, and into the devastating lightning.

“Charge!” The half beastman followed close behind.

“Father!” The giant took a big step and started to run, vibrating the floor.

“Kill——!” More people shouted as they surged out.

“Dammit! They really were enticed by the body double, all of them are charging up.” Jia Sha was ashen; this was undoubtedly the worst outcome.

Zhen Jin couldn’t see the scene on the third floor, but after hearing everyone rallying and getting Jia Sha’ confirmation, he immediately became terrified and promptly shouted: “What are you doing? You’re rebelling! You want to become an enemy of a templar knight and a Heavenly Emperor Sheng Ming priest, do you want to become wanted criminals? Do you want to be condemned? The lightning will strike you all down!”

As if proving his words, at the next moment, someone in the monstrous youngster’s group was struck by lightning and immediately died!

However, everyone had foreseen this, thus none of them stopped or even slowed down.

Their charging speed even increased somewhat.

They shouted and yelled, at that moment, they looked like a beast group of people!

“Why, why is it like this?!” Jia Sha flew into a rage out of humiliation.

At that moment, he seemed to return to the past.

He seemed to see that mob of people who rebelled against their feudal lord.

“You are inferior people! Weeds, worms! Why can’t you recognize reality?”

“Without me, you would have been swallowed up by beast groups. I also brought you into the alchemy factory! Are you biting the hand that feeds you?!”

“The strong rule the weak, the upper class rules the lower class, this is heaven’s law and earth’s principles! Yet you want to rebel against it?”

“What can you rebel with? In the end, you will die, all of you will die!”

As if responding to his voice, the chaotic lightning continued to wreak havoc.

Kacha!

Fei She’s eyelids trembled, after the lighting fell, he reacted.

Just a step from him, a muscular companion was turned to dust. He lost his life, for without battle qi or magic, he couldn’t resist the lightning. The body double was like this, let alone the others?

“Run for it, run for it!”

Fei She constantly aroused his strength while shouting vengefully in his heart: “President Zi Di, I will avenge you!”

Despite being like this, because he was obese, he still lagged behind.

But he wasn’t the last one.

The old Cang Xu did his best to run, however he was still last. As he watched the distance between the group and him increase, he panted for breath and sighed: “My old arms and legs really make this difficult.”

One by one, the monstrous youngster’s comrades were felled by lightning.

At that moment, it wasn’t strength, but luck that determined their life.

From this, the charge seemed crueler instead.

But no one retreated!

Because they didn’t charge for glory, but to survive. Instead of following behind some great character, they did it for themselves.

The lightning was like a baptism, a spiritual baptism.

As they got closer to the fourth floor’s stairs, their complexions revealed malevolence, excitement, zealotry, and brutality.

The brutality was enigmatic.

Hearing everyone’s footsteps get closer, Zhen Jin was thoroughly alarmed.

“Priest! This isn’t encouraging!”

“The lightning hasn’t killed most of them. Most crucially, the body double is still alive!”

“I am telling you; he is very powerful, and very terrifying! I watched him easily massacre a leopard leap with my own eyes!”

“Of course, the massacre was also sadistic. To him, it was effortless and a small demonstration of his impressive skills!”

“Hurry up, open the teleportation gate so we can escape!”

“Shut up for me! Rubbish!!” Jia Sha also looked malevolent, and equal parts furious and horrified.

But he couldn’t do anything.

He still couldn’t open the teleportation gate, if he could, he would have done so already!

“Almost there, almost there.”

“This group of wretched sinners! I shouldn’t have expected anything from them, I shouldn’t have saved them, or used divine spells to amplify or treat them!”

“Guardian templar knights, go down and block the fourth floor stairs, let no one charge in.”

“I need time, I only need a bit of time!”

But the four guardian templar knights showed a rare hesitation.

“Go now, I won’t make you throw your lives away!” Jia Sha also bellowed while simultaneously using his authority to remove a crucial limitation.

The four guardian templar knights were ecstatic, they no longer hesitated as they blocked the stairs.

Apprehensive about the lightning, they never stepped into the stairwell.

From the beginning, the monstrous youngster was at the forefront, and he was the first to crash into the guardian templar knights.

Without the slightest hesitation, he looked up and crashed into the four templar knights head-on.

Bang!

With a dull echo, the monstrous youngster was unexpectedly repelled and ruthlessly thrown to the third floor’s tiles.

“Battle qi!” The monstrous youngster was not lightly wounded as he quickly got up with red eyes.

That’s right, at that moment, the four guardian templar knights glittered with the radiance of battle qi.

They all had iron level strength, and as four iron level knights, they could form an extremely firm defensive line together.

For a long time, Mystifying Monster Island prohibited low level magic and battle qi, while high level magical artifacts were weakened.

But now, Jia Sha seemed to have lifted the restriction on the fourth floor, allowing the four guardian templar knights to use their battle qi.

Battle qi……once the monstrous youngster pursued that very thing, because battle qi was a qualitative change that could help him kill magic beasts stronger than him.

And now, since the enemy controlled battle qi, he was a monster restrained by it.

“Iron level battle qi, iron level equipment……” The monstrous youngster caressed his scorpion carapace. His carapace was at the silver level, however without the support of battle qi, a hole was cut into it.

The monstrous youngster coldly snorted, and stood up again.

Now that he was in this situation, what could he still do?

He charged again!

Halfway up the stairs, red light emerged from his body, transforming his head into that of a lizard’s that sprayed a large blob of corrosive liquid.

The acid shot at the four templar knights.

“Be careful!”

“That is green lizard acid.”

A templar knight shouted, he instantly recognized the ins and outs of the young man’s attack, and quickly lifted his shield.

The shield completely blocked the acid, the acid gurgling and causing white smoke to rise as it corroded the shield’s surface.

The shield still remained strong.

To thoroughly corrode the shield, at least a hundred more globs of acid were needed!

Time wouldn’t allow that.

It was also known that the stairwell up to the fourth floor from the third was also being devastated by the chaotic lightning.

The monster youngster continued to charge.

He undid the lizard form, and as red light flowed away, his head had become a bat monkey’s.

The next moment, the bat monkey howled, the strong soundwave rippling through the air could be seen by the naked eye.

The soundwave rushed into the fourth floor, devastating the templar knights’ eardrums.

But the templar knights did not fall, and with iron-like bodies, none even quivered.

Iron battle qi!

At this crucial moment, they used iron battle qi to protect their eardrums.

Although the soundwave did not affect them, they did hurt Zong Ge and the big guy, who both blocked their ears in pain.

“How vile!” The monstrous youngster was forced to undo his monkey head.

At best, the young man could only amplify the sound wave attack in a certain direction, he could not stop it from proliferating all around him, however.

Continuing to use it would instead bring a calamity to those on his side.

Regardless of whether it was the acid or the sound wave, both were ineffective.

Yet, the monstrous youngster still kept charging at the templar knights.

He raised his silver blade while simultaneously stimulating his muscles to ignite it with electricity.

The blade ferociously chopped down as electricity lit its tip!

The four templar knights acted in concert, two raised their shields to obstruct the silver spider blade, while the other two thrusted their iron swords from both sides at the monstrous youngster.

As the silver blade hit the shields, the templar knights’ two swords also hit the young man’s scorpion carapace.

The young man shouted as he swung his other silver blade.

A templar knight shield went askew, exposing a weak point in the defensive line.

The young man suddenly undid his silver blade and transformed his left hand into a bear claw.

The bear claw looked like molten iron, and was incomparably hot. It exploited the weak point and stabbed the foremost templar knight!

The templar knight had neither shield nor iron sword to protect him on time, thus he was forced to concentrate his battle qi on his own abdomen.

Iron colored light immediately emerged from the skin of his abdomen.

The youngster’s silver level bear claw was unable to penetrate the iron colored light!

But the templar knight was unable to offset its immense strength and was beaten back.

An immense crack formed in the four templar knights’ defensive line.

The monster youngster’s eyes suddenly shined, he took that chance to charge into the gap.

But the other three templar knights used their swords and shields to push back the youngster.

The young man’s legs transformed into the legs of a strong life white rhinoceros, giving him a strong stance.

For a split second, both sides sank into a contest of strength.

Chapter 209: The Most Deeply Hidden Person

The young man was defeated!

After two breaths of deadlock, the young man was beaten back once again, and fell down the flight of stairs.

The monstrous youngster quickly stood up again, and looked up at the four templar knights, his heart felt powerless.

It was different from the first time, the second time the young man had charged up, he had exhausted his full strength, yet was still defeated.

“This is the power of battle qi!”

Human bodies were far weaker than beast ones, but with the protection of battle qi, they could block the silver level bear claws and greatly increase their bodily essence, allowing them to withstand the monstrous youngster’s strength.

Sound wave attacks were also blocked by battle qi, and were useless.

The four templar knights were only iron levels, and only had iron battle qi. If it was silver level battle qi, it could be used to both protect the body while simultaneously imbuing their weapons and armor, making them even stronger.

“It seems that the fourth floor doesn’t restrain battle qi, as long as we charge through, we can also use battle qi!” Zong Ge rushed over and witnessed the young man getting punted down in its entirety.

“Correct.” The monstrous youngster took a breath, and charged for a third time.

Unlike before, this time he had Zong Ge behind him.

Liquid acid sprayed onto shields and swords clashed with each other, the four templar knights’ coordinated defense knocked back the monstrous youngster back again.

Then under the youngster enormous shadow, Zong Ge charged.

But he had lost all of his armor, and against four pairs of hands, he had to retreat wounded after the first clash.

“Father, let me through!” The big guy ran over and saw the monstrous youngster falling onto the floor, causing him to become exceedingly indignant.

“Be careful! Protecting yourself is vital!” The monstrous youngster’s face changed as he promptly warned.

But the big guy puffed out his chest, stepped onto the stairs, and with his hands, pried open the door.

“Courting death!” The four templar knights exploded in anger, and two of them swung their battle qi infused swords at the big guy’s heart. The other two lifted their shields to protect themselves and to block the big guy’s attacks.

If the battle qi empowered swords pierced his heart, the big guy would certainly die.

At that critical moment, the monstrous youngster rushed over and yanked the big guy’s clothes back.

The big guy suddenly jerked back, causing the templar knights’ swords to puncture his chest, but not reach his heart.

As the big guy tumbled to the ground, the monstrous youngster continued to attack.

At the same time, Zong Ge’s eyes gleamed, he leapt into the air, used the big guy’s head to reach a higher altitude, then dove at the four templar knights.

“I don’t think so!” At that crucial juncture, the four templar knights swiftly responded.

Three of them dealt with the monstrous youngster, while the last intercepted the half beastman in the air.

The young man and Zong Ge were beaten back again!

The increase to battle strength battle qi empowerment gave was too much.

During this deadlock, other companions charged up the stairs.

They wanted to go up, but the young man shouted at them to stop, “Don’t be impulsive!”

With just their human flesh, these people would be reduced to ghosts by the templar knights and sacrifice themselves in vain, they could not help the situation in any way.

“Dammit!” Zong Ge’s wound was constantly bleeding, but he ignored it.

His eyes glowered at the four templar knights, his mind burning with battle intent, “How can we charge through?”

If he had battle qi, these four templar knights wouldn’t be worth looking at.

Even if he didn’t have battle qi, if this battle was on equal grounds, he had many ways of dealing with their joint attacks.

“On the one hand, the fourth floor doesn’t restrict battle qi. On the other hand, they have the advantage of terrain!” Zong Ge analyzed.

“The flight of stairs is very narrow, War Merchant was a goblin, and was the only one in the control hub, thus these stairs were a comfortable size for him.

“But we humans have to go up one at a time, and cannot attack as two.”

The sturdy stairs could not be destroyed, and they were too narrow.

The monstrous youngster could only continue to charge, and they couldn’t use their advantage of numbers.

Previously, they had relied on their position to safely defend against the attacking beast groups. But now they wanted to overcome this very barrier, and attack the fourth floor.

Snap!

Lightning suddenly crashed down, striking a comrade.

The comrade was turned to ash, and the closeness of his death devastated everyone.

They had to force their way up, otherwise if they stayed on the third floor, the lightning would strike them sooner or later!

But charging one by one only played into the enemy’s strength.

They were at their wits’ end.

Everyone’s heart sank into hopelessness.

“I have a way!” Yet at that moment, Zong Ge suddenly spoke. With bright eyes, he gazed at the big guy.

“What is it?” Someone immediately asked.

Zong Ge pointed at the blockhead, “He is a human catapult, let him throw us!”

Everyone stared blankly, this was barbarous stratagem!

But the monstrous youngster’s heart was moved, “A good idea!”

They were limited by the stairs, and could not use their numbers. But by using this method, they could rush the stairs at the same time.

The monstrous youngster was the strongest of them, but he couldn’t stand against the combined attacks of the four templar knights alone.

If someone was working with him, even if it was only a little, it could give the monstrous youngster the time and space, this was worth doing!

The blockhead wouldn’t listen to anyone else but the youngster’s commands.

“Child, we are depending on you. You must throw accurately!” The monstrous youngster reminded.

The big guy took a deep breath, them slapped his chest, “Watch me father!”

The monstrous youngster charged up the stairs again.

Zong Ge walked up to the giant, “Throw me first!”

The big guy bellowed, grabbed the half beastman, then threw him.

Ba thunk.

The half beastman flew into the ceiling, then fell to the floor.

“Good throw!” The half beastmanm badly battered, quickly stood back up, then walked back to the big guy, “Be a bit more accurate!”

The big guy threw him again.

This time it was on target.

The monstrous youngster was in the middle of the stairs, and when he saw that, he immediately charged.

He and Zong Ge coordinated implicitly, and for the first time, the four templar knights wavered.

But they had no time to increase their gains as Zong Ge was already falling.

After all, while he was in the air, it was very difficult to use his strength.

Zong Ge ran back to the giant, battle intent surging turbulently, and roared, “Continue throwing me, and not just me, the others too. We have to keep up our offensive, otherwise the other side’s defense will hold.”

“Block them!” Jia Sha bellowed, he was growing impatient and restless.

Thanks to his keen battlefield insight, Zong Ge had found the fault that would collapse their defenses.

Naturally, the four templar knights thought about killing the big guy for the greatest insurance.

They immediately used battle skills.

A moment later, a bright blade shot out.

The big guy dodged at once, and the blade light glanced at his waist, causing blood to bubble out.

The big guy snarled, and his eyes went red, a warning that he was about to go crazy.

“We’re finished!” Many people cried to themselves.

“Child!” The monstrous youngster roared.

The big guy stared blankly, then by some miracle, he regained his clarity.

He snarled again, and his movements accelerated. He grabbed Zong Ge again, and like a stone, threw him at the enemy.

One after the other, the comrades in arms flew into the air and charged at the four templar knights in an unending offensive.

With all of his strength, the monster youngster tangled with the four templar knights.

For a moment, the templar knights were distracted by the offensive and didn’t have the leisure to deal with the big guy.

Finally, the monstrous youngster swept through and opened up a gap. A comrade passed through the gap and fell onto the fourth floor.

However, before he could stand, he was impaled through the stomach.

It was Zhen Jin!

Zhen Jin had already killed the person, but his hand didn’t stop thrusting and he dotted the corpse with holes.

“Don’t forget about me!”

“With me here, how can you be arrogant? Do you think that if you get into the fourth floor that everything will be fine?”

I am a templar knight, a templar knight!”

“Ahaha, you will have the honor of dying by my hand.”

“Do you recognize reality? You dregs! Your rebellion shall be ended by me, why didn’t you obediently listen to my commands?”

Zhen Jin abused the corpse while wildly shrieking his venting feelings.

He burst into laughter, for previously, the monster youngster’s and others’ assault made him nervous and fearful, now it had been superseded by the ecstasy of victory and pride.

At that moment, everyone was focused on the stairs.

When they heard Zhen Jin’s braying, it just stimulated their battle intent further.

“Charge, keep charging!” Zong Ge bellowed repeatedly.

The big guy grabbed more companions, and kept tossing them.

“Don’t, don’t throw me.” Cang Xu was forced towards the big guy by the crowd.

The blockhead’s eyes were flushed red, and had already mechanized the motion of throwing people, thus he didn’t hear Cang Xu’s cries. He grabbed Cang Xu and threw him.

The old scholar was thin, thus his velocity was greater.

“How terrible!” When the monstrous youngster saw this, he promptly exerted himself in an attempt to save Cang Xu’s life.

But a templar knight used a battle skill and beat back the young man.

The old scholar wailed as he flew up the stairs, and towards the templar knights’ swords.

“That’s the moment!” Zong Ge had been waiting for this opportunity, and he suddenly jumped.

The young man had fallen down during this, and slammed his palm into Zong Ge’s soles.

Zong Ge accelerated dramatically and attacked upwards.

At that moment, the templar knights were reorganizing their footing.

The combined attack of Cang Xu and Zong Ge was very swift, and the four templar knights could only intercept one of them.

They all elected independently to attack Zong Ge.

Zong Ge fought bravely and didn’t retreat.

Swords swung towards him, and with great determination on his face, the half beastman raised his arm and used it as a shield.

Psssh.

Blood sprayed from Zong Ge’s severed arm!

He seized that opportunity to rush to the fourth floor.

“As long as I set foot on the fourth floor, I can use my battle qi!” Zong Ge roared madly to himself.

“I don’t think so!” A templar knight shouted, and used his shoulder to collide with Zong Ge.

Zong Ge’s eyes were sluggish, and the templar knight stopped his charge and forced him to look on helplessly as he fell back.

But as he fell, he used his remaining hand to grab the templar knight who had bashed him.

The two tumbled down the stairs together.

By the time the two finished tumbling, Zong Ge had already twisted the templar knight’s neck during the fall.

At the same time, Cang Xu fell onto the tiles of the fourth floor.

Zong Ge stood up, he was soaked in blood, and turning a blind eye to his missing arm, he shot flames at the three templar knights.

When the three templar knights saw the bloodied lion, they all took a step back involuntarily.

“Don’t be afraid. I have already succeeded!” Jia Sha suddenly said.

A moment later, teleportation was turned on.

“Haha, priest, well done!! We are victorious in the end, let’s leave these people here to die!” Zhen Jin was ecstatic.

While he said that, he leapt at the unarmed and defenseless scholar, his sword aimed at Cang Xu’s chest.

When he saw that, even someone as tough as Zong Ge couldn’t help but shudder.

This included the monstrous youngster, all of them despaired.

Cang Xu had just stood up when his heart was skewered.

Zhen Jin malevolently laughed at Cang Xu, “Old fool, on what basis did you dare come up here? Did you come up here to receive death?” Hehehe!”

Zhen Jin’s eyes widened, he wanted to enjoy Cang Xu’s panic and helplessness as he approached death’s door.

But neither happened.

Cang Xu instead smiled.

Ping.

A moment later, a gush of magic power undulated on the fourth floor.

Astonished, Zhen Jin was struck away by the magic power.

Jia Sha and the three templar knights were likewise astonished.

Cang Xu pulled out the rapier from his heart, “Ah, I can finally use magic.”

From his chest, a single strand of blood leaked out.

So little blood came out!

Cang Xu wiped his hand across his chest, and the wound was sewn back together.

“You…you…you……” Zhen Jin crawled up, he looked incomparably frightened.

Jia Sha tenaciously gazed at Cang Xu, his face ashen, “You are a necromancer!”

The three templar knights were stunned silent, and they suddenly realized their blunder.

The weak and old scholar had been the most deeply hidden person.

An iron level necromancer!!

Chapter 210: The Swindler and The Monster

When some of the people on the stairwell saw Cang Xu use necromancy, they were also alarmed.

It was when they just landed on Mystifying Monster Island.

There was not enough food, thus the first mate assembled the first exploration team.

Cang Xu had volunteered.

Someone secretly disdained him, “That old man, does he not know himself? Instead of obediently hiding in camp, he wants to travel into the rainforest, he simply is courting death!”

……

Everyone that had been pursued by the scorpion group was at the end of their rope.

Bai Ya was wounded and unconscious, and the rest berated Cang Xu.

“It was because he saved you that Bai Ya was harmed!” One of them grabbed the old man by the collar and lifted him up.

Cang Xu coldly snorted, then suddenly waved his arm.

With a pow, the exploration team member’s grip was broken.

The exploration team member was caught off guard, and they recoiled back two steps. He looked at Cang Xu with bewilderment, the old man’s strength seemed to have gone against his expectations.

……

In a cave, Cang Xu had recounted his life to the body-double youngster.

“I occupied a position of power and at first, no one thought highly of me. They believed that I was extremely feeble, that I was only a simple scholar, and that one assassination could end my life. But in reality, I held the position for over thirty years.”

“He was called Lan Tu and he daydreamed all day of becoming a knight. He was my most trusted aide and assistant. After being seduced by the feudal lord’s third young miss, the youngster decided to betray me. He accused me and claimed that I was a dangerous criminal who had been hiding for many years, that I had malice towards the fief and the feudal lord’s entire family, and that I had been secretly conspiring against them.”

“You can eat me, Lord Zhen Jin!’

……

Everyone stopped their footsteps as the three templar knights fixed their attention on Cang Xu.

The teleportation gate was emitting a blue light.

Jia Sha had both succeeded and failed.

Because Cang Xu stood in front of the teleportation gate.

Zhen Jin got up, the rapier in his hand was clearly trembling.

Jia Sha’s voice broke the weird serenity, “Hehe, I didn’t expect that the final winner would actually be you! Hmph, an insignificant iron level.”

Honestly speaking, Cang Xu’s life level wasn’t that great, he was only an iron level. He belonged to the lower levels of cultivation ranks.

However, right now, both sides had depleted too much of their strength, and were approaching their limits.

The sudden reveal of Cang Xu’s iron level strength and his abundant magic power had instantly overturned the entire war situation.

Jia Sha deeply sighed, “You win, congratulations, Cang Xu. Now you can escort me and Zhen Jin through the teleportation gate. In the future, even if imperial might surrounds you, you can use us as negotiation chips.”

Zhen Jin’s face changed, then he yelled, “I am the Bai Zhen Clan’s sole heir, I. I can pay the ransom, a generous ransom!”

Below, everyone’s hearts jumped, they sensed things were anything but reassuring.

The teleportation gate was already opened.

Cang Xu was already on the fourth floor, no one could stop him.

He could go through the teleportation gate and escape with his life!

A moment prior, everyone sincerely believed that Cang Xu wouldn’t abandon his companions. But in the wake of Cang Xu exposing himself as a necromancer, everyone’s confidence in him took a dive.

This was what necromancers were!

In the empire, and even every other continent had long propagated that necromancers were callous to the extreme, killed people like flies, lacked all humanity, and were dangerous criminals that played with souls.

Now the focus of everyone’s eyes, Cang Xu smiled, then tilted sideways to look at Jia Sha, “You deserve to be called a Sheng Ming templar priest, that even at this stage, you still seek to grasp victory from defeat.”

“……”

“Unfortunately, you don’t understand. Regardless of whether one is upper or lower class, regardless of whether one is a priest or a criminal, everyone has a heart. Even I do…a necromancer with an impaled heart.”

Just as he said that, Cang Xu ignored Jia Sha’s dramatic change in expression as he attacked again.

Necromancy was cast, causing the three templar knights to freeze, and their movements became stiff.

Using this opportunity, the monstrous youngster gave a rallying cry and crashed into the three knights, finally ascending to the fourth floor!

“Great job, Cang Xu!” The young man burst into laughter.

Cang Xu smiled back.

Plop.

A moment later, the young man spat out some blood, the unbearable pain caused him to kneel on the ground.

Cang Xu’s expression changed.

“What are you doing? Cang Xu!” Soon after, Zong Ge charged up and shielding the young man.

Now on the fourth floor, he finally regained control, and all over him, there was a silver light.

Silver battle qi!

This half beastman was a silver level cultivator.

“Hold on, it wasn’t Cang Xu, I am the reason.” The young man intervened on behalf of Cang Xu.

After being struck by lightning, the young man had already realized what the problem was.

“My transformations caused this!”

“I changed my bloodline to that of those remodeled magic beasts. Those magic beasts were all incomplete beings, they needed the magic prohibition environment to exist.”

“The fourth floor doesn’t have the magic prohibition, and my compound transformations into those remodeled magic beasts are no longer stable!”

The young man tried to lessen his transformations.

Sure enough, the pain rapidly receded.

Only after he turned back into the appearance of Zhen Jin, retaining just the scorpion shell, did the sharp pain alleviate enough that he was able to endure it for the time being.

He slowly stood up, and his body too radiated with silver light.

Like Zong Ge, he had also awakened his internal battle qi.

The youngster spared a moment to sigh ruefully: the battle qi he had so looked forward to was once again within his grasp on the control hub’s fourth floor.

The three templar knights retreated back to Jia Sha.

The body double youngster and the half beastman were silver fighters, and with Cang Xu, an iron level necromancer, Jia Shan and Zhen Jin couldn’t turn the tides.

The overall situation had been decided!

The others quickly ascended up the stairs, including the big guy, and all of them piled into the fourth floor.

“Zi Di!” The young man cried out.

“Don’t get any closer, or else I shall skewer her head!” Zhen Jin clutched Zi Di’s neck and dragged her up, simultaneously using his other hand to point the rapier at Zi Di’s chin.

Zi Di was in terrible shape, she was covered in cuts and her blood drenched her apprentice mage gown.

She was deathly pale, without blood. Her once pure and glittering amethyst like eyes had become dull.

“Let her go, that’s my president!” Fei She growled, his mind was in complete disarray.

“As long as you agree to let me go, I will release your beloved woman!” Zhen Jin tenaciously stared at the body double youngster, half threatening, half begging.

But at that moment, his movements froze, as if he had fallen into a world of ice and snow, his entire spirit had gone rigid.

“Now!” Cang Xu urged, he had used magic on the sly.

A soundless cast!

The necromancer had a deep mastery of casting.

The body double youngster suddenly stepped forth, and like a human arrow, he reached Zhen Jin in the blink of an eye.

Thump.

Silver battle qi flashed.

Zhen Jin coughed out blood as he tumbled away like a sack. The rapier in his hand fell on the tiles with a clang and slid away.

The body double youngster embraced Zi Di, the latter’s heavy injuries caused the young man’s heart to sink.

He promptly undid Zi Di’s handbag around her waist and took out healing medicine.

The young man used a trembling hand to sprinkle the medicine on Zi Di’s wounds and pour it into Zi Di’s mouth.

But the medicine simply wasn’t enough.

“Lord President!!!” Fei She also wanted to run over, however he tumbled halfway there, battered and exhausted.

As if sensing the young man’s warm hug, Zi Di slowly opened her eyes.

“Zi Di!” The young man was overjoyed.

Zi Di weakly smiled to the young man, and barely opened her mouth, “I…knew you would come back.”

“Sorry I came late, sorry I came late.” The young man lowered his head and wept, he was brimming with self-blame.

“No, I should be the one who says sorry. I…should have said sorry earlier. I am not your princess, I am only…a swindler.” In the last moments of her life, Zi Di finally had the courage to confess to the young man.

Her heart was filled with both love and remorse.

“Forgive me forgive me…” Zi Di gazed at the young man, she wanted to extend her hand and caress the youngster’s cheek.

However, darkness surged, and her dusky field of view went black.

The girl’s hand stretched out halfway, then flopped down.

The young man’s heart trembled with horror as he reached out to hold the young lady’s hand.

Ice cold.

Regardless of whether it was the girl’s hand or the young man’s heart.

“No, no, no…” The young man foolishly stammered, tears rolling from his eyes.

“Don’t sleep, you can’t sleep, Zi Di.”

“Wake up, wake up and look at me.”

The young man kept repeating himself, but nothing responded back.

The eyes of the girl in his arms were shut, and she no longer breathed.

“Please, please there has to be a way.”

“There must be something, there has to be something that can save Zi Di!”

“Right, the heart magic core. As long as turn Zi Di’s flesh into that of a Strong Life White Rhinoceros, she can avoid death!”

But the young man failed.

Perhaps it was possible, but he had never tried and didn’t know what to do. The red light couldn’t separate itself from his body, let along extend itself to Zi Di’s.

“Help me, someone help me now!” The young man exasperatedly beseeched his companions.

Many of them couldn’t bear to look at him.

“I can!” Cang Xu ran over.

Fei She also came to Zi Di’s side, then when he saw that Zi Di was dead, his legs gave out and he fell to the ground, howling in grief.

Cang Xu quickly examined Zi Di, then shook his head, “She is already dead.”

“But you must have some method, right?” The young man looked up at Cang Xu.

Facing the young man’s eyes, Cang Xu gave an indeterminate nod, “Her body is already dead, but as for her soul, I can still do something.”

“I can try to transform her soul into a ghost.”

“Gho, ghost?!” Fei She’s eyes widened with fright.

The young man’s expression also changed.

“This is the only thing I can do. At the same time, I am also very unsure about it. Not everyone’s soul can be transformed into a ghost, and making things more difficult, Miss Zi Di has depleted too much of her spirit. To be honest, there is a miniscule chance she’ll be able to be reborn as a ghost.” Cang Xu stated frankly.

He gave the body-double youngster the power to choose.

Was it better for Zio Di’s corpse to be desecrated by necromancy for that slim chance, or should he let Zi Di pass away in peace?

A mere second later, the young man issued his decision, “Then do it, Cang Xu, you must do everything you can!”

“Of course, my Lord. I sincerely hope that I have the power to help you.” After saying that, Cang Xu began to chant and use necromancy.

However, when the necromancy flowed into Zi Di, she didn’t react.

Ten seconds, twenty, thirty seconds, one minute, two minutes…

Cang Xu sighed, “I failed.”

Fei She slumped down, paralyzed.

The young man was a sluggish as stone, as his entire being fell into the abyss of despair.

On the other side, the fight Zong Ge was dominating was also spinning to its end.

He had hacked the three templar knights to death, captured Jia Sha, who was out of divine power, and Zhen Jin, who had been seriously wounded by the youngster, surrendered willingly, his fighting spirit gone.

“Don’t kill me, my father will pay a great sum to redeem me. All of you will become wealthy!” Zhen Jin shouted.

“Hehehe.” But Jia Sha actually laughed, “Even if you win, you won’t dare to kill me, because I control the teleportation gate. Do you think you can pass through the teleportation gate? Without my permission, you cannot escape!”

Everyone was startled.

“He is right!” Zhen Jin promptly shrieked, “Not long ago, as the tower spirit was dying, Zi Di’s and his spirit fought over the authority. With Zi Di dead, Jia Sha won, he is now our sole hope to escape this place!”

“That…” Zong Ge and the others hesitated.

“Don’t forget, I am also a magician. Since your spirits eroded the authority here, I can do the same.” Cang Xu interjected.

Everyone had just begun to smile when they were extinguished by Jia Sha, “You have the ability to, but the time has passed! The magic power reservoir has been spent, the lightning that keeps striking the third floor is consuming your slot to escape. Hehehe, by the time you break in and obtain the authority, the magic power reservoir will have been long drained. The best way to escape is cooperating with me!”

“Don’t forget me, priest, don’t forget about me!” Zhen Jin reminded at once.

“Shut up!” Tripleblade slapped Zhen Jin to the ground.

Zhen Jin’s head hit the tiles, bloodying his skin with cuts.

After being disciplined, Zhen Jin finally shut his mouth.

Zong Ge’s expression was as heavy as water, the situation was far more critical than he had expected, “We can all be teleported? How many can be teleported? Can you prove what your words?”

Jia Sha glanced at Cang Xu, the latter had already begun to use his magic power to erode authority, “Cang Xu can prove it, as long as he occupies a point of authority, his spirit can check how much magic power is left in the reservoir.”

A half minute later, Cang Xu sighed, “He’s right. There is only a little magic power, it can only teleport five people.

Everyone was in an uproar.

There were many present, far more than five.

Jia Sha broke out in giggles, “You better talk it over well, who will leave, who will stay?”

For a moment, the expression of many changed, the mood shifting to a more subdued state.

“Don’t let that old bastard stir things up!” The body-double youngster shouted.

Everyone gazed at him, he was walking towards them, his face stained with both blood and tears. But his eyes were still resolute and clear, “We will leave together, if we can’t, then we will die together!!”

The direct and efficient declaration and unflinching look caused everyone’s hearts to relax.

“That’s right, that’s how it should be!”

“We shall listen to you, your Lordship.”

“To die together, to live together!!”

“Hahaha, even if we die, we will still profit by begin buried with these bigshots.”

Everyone shouted, all of them approved of the youngster’s words.

“These people are all insane, they are actually listening to that monster!” Zhen Jin’s eyes widened, fear was filling them. But because Tripleblade had slapped him, he could only madly roar in his heart, he didn’t dare to make a sound.

Jia Sha’s expression changed, “Are you certain? Are you sure you want to give up this last chance?”

“Hehehe.”

“I urge everyone to calm down a bit.”

“Why don’t you think about your wives, your children, your parents, or perhaps the burdens and expectations you are shouldering?”

“Even though most of you will die, some of you can survive, why do you want to give that up?”

“Even dead men can request many things from the survivors.”

Like a sharp arrow, the priest’s words pierced everyone’s hearts.

“Shut up, if it weren’t for you coveting the divine artifact, we could have left already.” The body-double youngster walked over, hate filled his face.

Jia Sha smiled, “What use is it in speaking about that now?”

“You only want to see us strife with each other, and fight amongst ourselves. Since you hold teleportation authority, the final decision on who can or cannot escape lies with you.” As the young man said this, he drew a dagger.

It was Zi Di’s dagger, it had mainly been used for collecting medicine, and was of extraordinary quality.

In the spider forest, Zi Di had tossed this very dagger to the body-double youngster so he could free himself.

“What are you doing…ack!” Jia Sha suddenly scowled.

The young man ruthlessly thrusted the dagger into his belly.

“What am I doing? Taking vengeance of course! Not only for Zi Di, but for everyone’s animosity.” At that moment, the young man looked quite terrifying, he looked like the blackened surface of lava that, at any time, could wrathfully rush out and swallow everything!

Sensing the youngster’s killing intent, Jia Sha stopped pretending.

What kind of person was Jia Sha?

How could he compromise with these lowly underclass people?

He only wanted to enjoy watching them tear each other to shreds as he lay dying.

Cang Xu then spoke, “Jia Sha has deceived us, he is in an extremely terrible state, and has exhausted all of his potential and vitality. Without immense merits that would grant him at least a legend level divine spell treatment, he will soon die.”

Jia Sha shrugged his shoulders high and pointlessly smiled, “Then we shall die together.”

“Don’t worry, you won’t get off that easily.” The body-double youngster took a step back, then looked at all the others, “Next, you all should come take your revenge.”

Everyone stared blankly at him, but soon got his meaning.

“I will go first.” The closest companion quickly walked to Jia Sha, took the dagger, then stabbed him once

Jia Sha grunted, his eyes seemed to be shooting flames.

Immediately afterwards, another companion took over to stab him with the knife. Before leaving, he didn’t forget to spit on Jia Sha’s face.

The priest angrily shuddered all over, “You group of untouchables, untouchables!!”

Another companion walked over and stabbed Jia Sha.

The young man crossed his arms, supervising this to the side.

Finally, it was Bai Ya’s turn. The youngster trembled as he took the dagger, his eyes closed as he thrusted forth.

The dagger pierced Jia Sha’s body.

As death approached, Jia Sha nevertheless howled with laughter, “Your sins cannot be forgiven, you will all die, all of you must die. You will never obtain the Gods’ redemption, your souls will be cast into hell and the abyss…ack!”

His stunned expression froze on his face.

He rigidly gazed ahead, like he was seeing some unimaginable sight.

Everyone followed his gaze and looked yonder.

A moment later, everyone else was shocked dumb.

A ghost was unhurriedly shaking out of Zi Di’s corpse and wafting in the air.

“Zi Di!” After recovering, the youngster cheered with joy.

Fei She clapped Cang Xu’s back, “You did it, you did it!”

The clap staggered Cang Xu and he almost fell, shock spread across his face, “Her broken soul became a ghost, nigh impossible, unless…”

A possibility floated into his mind, and Cang Xu decided to ask, “Miss Zi Di, did you once cultivate necromancy?”

Chapter 211: Self-destruct

“What, what happened to me?” Zi Di was startled to find her body and limbs appeared to be a translucent gray color.

And on the ground, her pale body no longer breathed, because she had lost too much blood.

“So, I turned into a ghost?” Zi Di realized in her daze.

In that moment, Cang Xu’s voice inquired: “Miss Zi Di, did you also practice necromancy?”

Zi Di slightly stared, then she recalled her days at the Academy of Magic. Once, a suitor gave her some taboo knowledge——the soul devourer magic cultivation technique.

When her future was dark and when her hopeless aptitude had almost driven her insane, she embraced the soul devourer cultivation technique she had obtained by a fluke.

In the end, before using it for the first time, she saw that suitor killed on the spot because he cultivated necromancy.

Although Zi Di was also tested, perhaps because she only cultivated necromancy briefly and superficially, nothing was detected.

Thus, Zi Di was immensely frightened, and after that lesson, she no longer touched necromancy.

“I didn’t expect necromancy would indirectly save my life.” Zi Di whispered as she nodded to Cang Xu.

Cang Xu understood clearly: “As expected.”

He was not surprised that Zi Di could access knowledge pertaining to necromancy.

The school of necromancy was also one of the major schools of magic. Its founder was You Can.

When You Can first founded it, they only spread it to a few apprentices. Necromancy was always hidden away and developed slowly.

Eventually, it was exposed one day. Both the people and the gods were terrified!

The school of Necromancy targeted life, thus it naturally provoked hate. Necromancy also touched the secrets of the soul, and as this was the gods’ restricted domain, they immediately ordered its destruction.

As a legend, You Can had no choice but to hide and wander all over. They became the enemy of almost all people, and all gods.

As a way of rebelling, You Can began to scatter knowledge of necromancy and did their best to circulate it.

Necromancy’s original advantage was that it allowed those with limited aptitudes and talent to see new hope.

For a moment, necromancy bred like flies and continued to do so despite repeated prohibition. This consumed every major power’s energy while allowing the true necromancer elites to hide better.

“Many thanks Cang Xu, I definitely wouldn’t have been able to condense my soul into a ghost.” Zi Di expressed her gratitude, “Also, thank you for killing Jia Sha. I have now completed my contract with the tower spirit to kill that priest.”

After the contract was fulfilled, Zi Di’s spirit was no longer burdened, only by breaking away from this final shackle could she transform her soul into a ghost.

“Zi Di.” The body double youngster moved toward the ghost girl, with a complex expression he extended his hand, however he only touched air.

“Your Lordship……” The ghost girl Zi Di looked on helplessly as her fingers passed through his, and her heart filled with emptiness and disappointment.

However, the two continued to look at each other with deep love.

“Cough cough.” Cang Xu coughed twice and couldn’t help but warn, “You two, don’t forget we are still in danger, we should think of a way to escape this island.”

Met with Cang Xu’s warning, the girl suddenly reacted from her entangled emotions.

Zi Di’s face was solemn: “This is the most important place in War Merchant’s alchemy factory control hub. I will continue to erode the tower spirit and seize the authority here. I will find out if there is a way to escape this place soon? It is very possible War Merchant has elemental crystals in his storage that we can use to replenish the magic power reserve. With sufficient magic power, we can teleport away.”

Zong Ge walked over, his severed arm had already been hastily bandaged: “Actually we have another option, the flame dragon king. It's not out of the question, we may be able to negotiate with it. By giving up a portion of contribution, he can grant us a helping hand.”

“This……can only be considered as a last resort?”

“Previously, we really offended him.”

“He is a legendary dragon king; will he take us ants into consideration?”

“Perhaps, we can help him escape the seal.”

“Without us, I can still see him escaping.”

“Don’t say that, if we want to negotiate with him, won’t we need to deal with those golems first?”

Everyone whispered and discussed.

As Jia Sha was tormented to death, the teleportation door disappeared. The third floor still had chaotic lightning, while the second and first floor still had artificial magic beasts tearing at each other's throats.

Everyone could only find another path.

Zi Di began to invade the privileges again.

The process was very smooth and quick.

“Without Jia Sha hindering me and the tower spirit seeming to have withered away, my journey is unimpeded!” Zi Di quickly obtained complete teleportation privileges, weather control privileges, and more.

She first stopped the lightning, controlled the odor again, and took command over the artificial magic beasts the tower spirit controlled.

Under the beast groups’ watchful gaze, some people left the control hub while the body double youngster and several others continued to protect Zi Di while she continued to invade the privileges.

“I already control the magic power reserve, however there isn’t much magic power left.”

“I have just gained authority over the alchemy golems.”

“There are many warehouses here, there might be some elemental crystals hidden in them.”

Every piece of good news was transmitted from Zi Di’s mouth.

Zong Ge’s group had left the control hub and headed towards the warehouses that might have elemental crystals.

During their journey, they encountered alchemy golems and magical traps, all of which were under Zi Di’s control. Each of them moved quickly, it was like galloping through a flat prairie.

Waiting until the manpower came, the warehouse metal door then automatically opened with a rumble.

“This place has ore.”

“This place has processed alchemy materials, and even Obscure Gold!”

“These look like pieces of alchemy golems. I understand now, this is an alchemy golem’s module.”

“Good news, we found several boxes of magic scrolls.”

There were a few healing scrolls in the bunch, they were then quickly sent to the control hub.

On the fourth floor, the wounded used the scrolls to heal themselves. Zong Ge’s arm was also attached; however, he still couldn’t use it in the short term as it remained bandaged. Zi Di’s corpse also received strong treatment.

Cang Xu faintly shook his head: “That won’t work, her heart has already stopped beating, after being dead for too long, common treatment methods can only make a corpse intact, nothing more.”

“Her internal organs are already beginning to rot; she must be frozen to slow down the decay.”

As a necromancer, Cang Xu was also an expert at handling corpses, thus he was the one using the ice magic scroll.

Thus, Zi Di’s body was sealed in an icy coffin.

Bang rumble……

Another earthquake happened.

“Make haste, hurry up!”

“If this continues, the flame dragon king might escape his prison at any moment.”

“We should control that magma battlefield.”

The exploration team was becoming increasingly nervous as they raced against time to continue exploring.

Finally, they found their target.

“This should be an elemental crystal warehouse!”

However, everyone despaired, for the warehouse was empty.

By the wayside, there were one or two elemental crystals left on the ground.

“This passage leads directly to the magic power reserve; it seems the tower spirit has already replenished it with elemental crystals.”

“If we can’t replenish magic power, how can we escape?”

“Search! Keep searching.”

“Maybe War Merchant’s storage has an alchemy airship, we can’t say for sure.”

Everyone suffered a blow, but none of them abandoned hope.

Another door was opened through Zi Di’s authority.

This warehouse was filled with translucent white crystal pillars.

With difficulty, everyone discovered these white crystal pillars were not natural ore, but an alchemy product.

Zi Di was delighted at this discovery: “That is the tower spirit. Or to be more accurate, that is the medium the tower spirit is placed in. It’s like the relationship between the soul and the corporeal body.

Zi Di didn’t want to stay on the fourth floor.

She already obtained all privileges, no matter where she was, she could use them.

Very quickly, she personally came to the white crystal pillars.

“There are many databases here, the memory storage and written records here contain the secrets of War Merchant’s alchemy factory!” Saying this, Zi Di began to invade with her spirit.

The body double youngster also felt a rush of excitement as he thought of his magic core, those crystal pillars must contain information about it!

“I found the alchemy factory’s blueprint, there are three elemental crystal warehouses altogether.”

“Hold on, this is a records table. All of the elemental crystals have been consumed to replenish magic power.”

Zi Di’s reporting made the people happy for a moment, then concerned.

Tripleblade urged: “Search now, is there an airship tarmac?”

Zi Di shook her head: “No such installation, wait a minute, what is this?”

“I found records of War Merchant’s and his apprentice’s journeys……”

“An alchemy ship!”

“An alchemy ship is stored on this island, War Merchant sometimes used it to travel.”

Zi Di’s discovery made everyone cheer.

They didn’t have a way to replenish magic power, and hadn’t found an alchemy airship, but an alchemy ship was enough to sustain their hopes of escape.

“Warning, warning!”

“Tower spirit had been invaded by another spirit, intruder discovered, discovered all privileges have been lost……”

The tower spirit’s voice echoed again, causing everyone to become very nervous.

However, the tower spirit’s voice soon became intermittent, after constantly examining itself, it found all authority was in Zi Di’s hands.

“It seems the tower spirit really was dying.” Everyone relaxed for a breath before suddenly hearing the tower spirit’s voice once again.

“Incapable of counterattacking, self-destruct countdown initialized, database destruction initialized.”

Everyone turned pale with fright.

The immense crystal pillars also began to collapse. On their surface, countless cracks quickly spread and broke down, turning them into dust.

“The countdown has begun; this is the final self-destruct. As long as it isn’t War Merchant, no one can stop it!” Zi Di involuntarily shouted.

Chapter 212: Beast Knight

“We need to flee now!” Someone held their head.

“Don’t worry, we still have some time.” Cang Xu maintained his calm.

Zi Di subconsciously took a deep breath and her eyes seemed to flicker like gold coins: “Time is limited, not only do we need to leave alive, we need to do our best to acquire the greatest benefits! A reserve medium is in warehouse 12, move it here. Now!”

Everyone immediately acted.

The tower spirit’s reserve medium was a gigantic crystal ball that was many times taller than a person.

The crystal ball was pushed to the crystal pillars, then under Zi Di’s control, the tower spirit began to move.

More than half of the crystal pillars had collapsed already.

The tower spirit was too weak, and it could do nothing to resist as it was moved into the crystal ball.

Zi Di looked at the nearly collapsed and bamboo thin crystal pillars: “Only a third of the memory database has been transported with the tower spirit, one third has collapsed, and one third remains here. A real pity, these things are extremely valuable, however we can’t take them.”

“By researching these, can we learn the truth of Mystifying Monster Island?” Cang Xu asked.

“Certainly” Zi Di immediately moved, her spirit constantly pried into the crystal pillars.

Mystifying Monster Island really was War Merchant’s headquarters. When a small squadron from the Blood Light Punishment Institution suddenly attacked, War Merchant was caught unprepared. After barely sealing the flame dragon king, he finally died by a legendary weapon.

The Blood Light Punishment Institution’s saint levels also all died.

Both sides nearly destroyed each other.

“The third floor’s mysterious treasure is called the Bloodcore. War Merchant used it and the first floor’s Green Jade Golden Coffin to make batches of artificial magic beasts.” Zi Di’s words made the youngster’s heart jump.

The body double youngster was just about to ask Zi Di for his true origin, but after hearing that, he immediately altered his thinking and rapidly asked: “What information do you have on the Bloodcore? How did War Merchant make it? How can it be controlled?”

“I was just investigating that……” Zi Di tightly scowled, her spirit was always focused.

But at that moment, the crystal pillar collapsed in a bang, and turned into white dust that fell to the ground.

Everyone was dumbstruck.

After a few seconds, Zi Di took the lead and reacted first: “Hurry, transport everything that can be moved.”

“Abandon the raw materials that still need to be refined!”

“The alchemy golem components are too heavy; we don’t want them.”

“Does anyone still remember the goods in the plaza? We need those weapons!”

“The Bloodcore and Mermaid Fairy Tale are a must.”

“Don’t forget the boxes of magic scrolls and the tower spirit medium.”

“Hurry hurry hurry, move everything!”

The ghost girl’s high-pitched screeching voice immediately seized leadership from the body double youngster as it commanded with incomparable power.

Everyone rapidly acted.

Most of the treasures were concentrated in one place.

Zong Ge creased his brows: “Hold on, don’t forget about the flame dragon king. After the self-destruct, the alchemy magic formation will also break. If it escapes, we will be at a disadvantage. Even if we have a ship, we can’t escape far.”

Zi Di replied: “The alchemy factory’s self-destruct is extraordinary, not only will it completely destroy Mystifying Monster Island, it will also warp space, cause banishment, and have other effects. In other words, after the explosion, the ruins and slag will be banished to another plane nothing will remain in its original location.”

Zong Ge asked again: “Can the explosion kill the flame king dragon?”

Zi Di pursed her brows and hesitated: “I don’t know, after all I was only at the iron level in life, that’s all.”

“Let me chat with it.” The body double youngster said, “We killed the Sheng Ming priest, and are also holding a noble heir captive. If we can successfully negotiate with it, maybe it can take the guilt off of us. But the prerequisite to this, is we need to part with much of our spoils of war.”

Everyone’s eyes shined equally.

“If our guilt can be removed, that would be for the best.”

“Yes, no one wants to be hunted by the empire.”

“My Lord, I will come with you!”

The body double youngster shook his head: “No, I can move faster by myself. Most of the golems in the magma battlefield were War Merchant’s direct subordinates, without the alchemy factory's authority controlling them, it is very dangerous.”

“Zi Di, I need you to command these artificial magic beasts to fight for time and space so I can converse with the flame dragon king.

“No problem!” Zi Di immediately agreed.

The body double youngster entered the magma battlefield again.

Using the artificial magic beasts to attack the alchemy golems, the youngster hid behind a boulder in a remote corner, then he fished out the second Bloodcore.

This was a significant divine artifact and was naturally in his care.

His heart’s urge to swallow made him instinctively activate the Bloodcore in his heart, emitting red light.

The red light extended to the second Bloodcore, rapidly melting the latter and transforming it into a ball of red light.

After a moment, all of the red light returned.

As the two Bloodcores fused into one entity, the youngster’s Bloodcore faintly grew.

The incomparable desire to swallow that tormented the youngster also vanished completely.

“Bloodcore……”

There wasn’t time for the body double youngster to examine the Bloodcore’s new changes, because the artificial magic beasts were winning.

The youngster immediately appeared and ran to the edge of the magma.

“Flame dragon king!” He shouted, opening negotiations with the flame dragon king.

The negotiations didn’t go smoothly.

Seeing the youngster return, the flame dragon king immediately realized War Merchant was already dead. Someone as arrogant as it didn’t wish to divide the contribution with these small insects.

From its point of view, this was rational.

War Merchant’s death was caused by the Blood Light Punishment Institution’s squadron. The young knight’s group was simply here by chance, nothing more. If he wasn’t forced by circumstance, the youngster wouldn’t have tried to negotiate with it.

“You dregs don’t have a choice!”

“Do you really think you can negotiate with a king?”

“Hahaha, ridiculous! For what reason? Are you relying on your freedom?”

“Without my help, you will all die! Even if you don’t help me escape, I can still escape this alchemy formation, when that happens, you will face my fury!”

“Now, I order you to open this alchemy formation and grovel for forgiveness. Perhaps if I’m in a good mood, I will spare some of your lives. Hahaha.”

The negotiation with the flame dragon king was an utter failure.

The body double youngster was frustrated and worried.

This was a failure, if the self-destruct didn’t kill the flame dragon king, the latter would definitely demand the exploration team’s lives!

What should be done?

The Bloodcore thirsting impulse came again.

But this thirst wasn’t as strong as the last one.

The youngster’s heart was moved: “Perhaps, the Bloodcore can……”

According to his heart’s intuition, he began to use the Bloodcore.

The red light permeated through the alchemy formation and gradually covered the flame dragon king’s enormous body.

“What are you doing?”

“Hehehe, do you also think you can harm me?!”

“Lowly insect, you cannot imagine harming a grand legend.”

One minute later.

“No, no!”

“You cannot do this to me!”

“You wretched insect……good, good! I admit you are qualified to negotiate with me, we can speak now.”

The youngster was indifferent: “No, negotiations are unnecessary.”

After the red light completely covered the flame dragon king, it quickly melted its body.

The flame dragon king wrathfully roared and endlessly cursed the young knight.

Then, it disappeared.

It had completely transformed into a gigantic ball of red light and a massive pile of carbon ash.

As the carbon ash was quickly engulfed by the magma, the red light flowed back into the youngster’s new Bloodcore.

“I, I can actually use this Bloodcore to kill a legend level existence!” The youngster was endlessly shocked by this result.

Leaving the remaining artificial magic beasts and alchemy golems still fighting, the youngster quickly returned to everyone else.

“Negotiations have failed.” He stated.

This outcome made everyone’s heart drop; however, it wasn’t unexpected.

“Now, we can only hope the self-destruct explosion kills it.”

“At worst, it will be banished to a different plane!”

No one knew the flame dragon king was already dead.

The body double youngster opened his mouth, but in the end his heart sighed, and he didn’t speak of the Bloodcore.

Everyone carried the treasure and squeezed into three underground rail carts.

The rail carts quickly moved, and not long after, they stopped at an underwater dock.

Here, they saw the alchemy ship.

It looked like a gigantic blue-gold fish.

“It is called the Deep Sea Monster Fish!” With her complete authority, Zi Di easily led everyone through the passage and onto the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Passing by the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s exploration equipment, the youngster had a discovery: “As it turns out, this place is the desert oasis!”

He thought of the fierce battle that happened here, when he used the green lizard eyes to see the fat ball flying fish, he also saw the lake had a terrifying heat source at the bottom.

“So, that heat source was actually the Deep Sea Monster Fish!”

The Deep Sea Monster Fish dived through an underground river and rapidly escaped Mystifying Monster Island.

After getting ten nautical miles away, Mystifying Monster Island self-destructed.

The enormous explosion shook heaven and earth, causing a stream of water to cover the sky.

Space quickly shattered, sea water vanished, innumerable magic beast corpses were destroyed, and all of the island ruins were dragged into a black hole and banished to a different plane.

In the Deep Sea Monster Fish, everyone watched with trepidation.

After space reinstated itself and the seawater became serene, they didn’t see the king flame dragon.

“It seems it either died or was banished!”

“Woohoo! We survived.”

“How wonderful, we succeeded!!”

For a moment, the Deep Sea Monster Fish filled with wild joy.

But the ghost Zi Di was wobbling as she descended to the floor.

“Zi Di, what’s happening to you?” The youngster was incomparably nervous.

“She needs to rest immediately. It is like a tired person needing to sleep. In the end, she is only a newborn ghost, nothing more. Fortunately, she still has her body, it can allow her to recuperate for the time being.” Cang Xu replied.

Hearing Cang Xu’s words, the youngster relaxed. “Zi Di, rest well. I have so many things I want to say to you.”

“Me too.” The ghost girl caressed the youngster’s face, but she went through him.

She returned to her icy body and sank into hibernation.

Several months later.

On a tranquil sea surface, a strong radiance suddenly flashed.

A massive teleportation spell that covered a diameter of ten meters appeared from nowhere, then disappeared shortly after.

Three human characters floated in the air and looked down on the sea.

They all wore scarlet gowns, iron gloves, iron boots, and iron masks.

On the front and back of the blood red gowns, there were nine white-gold stars.

They came from the Blood Light Punishment Institution!

“Is this the place?” The leader’s life aura was at an awe-inspiring legend level.

“I am certain!” Another character with long black braids said, he emitted a gold level aura, and said with a male voice: “His scent is here, he absolutely came to this place!”

The leader nodded: “Then let me take a glimpse.”

The next moment, he used a divine spell to look back upon time. This divine spell could repeat the sounds and images of the past directly into the leader’s heart.

The leader’s body suddenly shook.

The shaking was keenly observed by the other two, making their hearts amazed. They had never seen their leader lose their self-control in this way.

After a moment, the legend level leader heavily said: “The divine spell was ineffective, some formidable power is thwarting my investigations.”

“I only barely heard some sounds.”

“I heard the frightful shouts of beast groups, the berating of a Sheng Ming priest, a noble surrendering, and a flame dragon king desperately roaring at death’s door.

The two others’ hearts quaked, and they lost their voice.

“What?!”

“My Lord, what is the meaning of this?”

“That’s right, my institution’s missing third squadron died here. The island that was once here——was Mystifying Monster Island! This was once War Merchant’s old nest!!”

Learning this information, the two characters lost their voices again.

“The alchemy great grandmaster War Merchant?!”

“Did the fight that erupted here sink Mystifying Monster Island and kill everyone?”

The leader was unsure about this as the divine spell entered its final phase.

With a sinking heart, he continued to listen attentively.

Quickly, he heard the vague and disjointed dialogue of some people.

“We should leave……”

“As for the captain, I can only acknowledge you.”

“No, to be more accurate, all of us acknowledge you, your Lordship.”

“But I am only a beast……”

“No, no one believes that. It was you who led us, and we accept you as our leader. You are a true knight!”

The dialogue was like a dreamy wind and quickly dissipated.

The leader sighed, knowing he couldn’t gain anything else; he stopped the divine spell.

“Some people survived, they might have obtained great spoils and War Merchant’s inheritance!” The leader stated.

“Who were they?” The two promptly asked.

The leader shook his head and said with extreme regret: “I cannot determine that……I can only determine they have already decided their chief, and that their chief has great prestige.”

“If your Lordship cannot determine it, I fear no one else in the empire can investigate any more information.” The two were very frustrated.

The leader coldly snorted: “The darkness will only shroud for a while, in the end, the light will pervade it. These people cannot hide forever, there will inevitably be a day when they are revealed.”

“We will continue our mission, you, return and report this information to the institution.”

“First let's order the arrest of these people!”

“Yes, my Lord. May I ask the code name of the wanted……”

Since they didn’t know the name, they used a code name. War Merchant was one such code name.

The leader thought of one: “Beast Knight.”

FINIS.

Chapter 213: The Young Captain

Book 2: Redemption of Righteousness

An alchemical ship swiftly sped across the depths of the ocean floor like a shot arrow.

It was the Deep Sea Montser Fish.
